Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n              unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), need\\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n            group_cnt = N - count;\\n        }\\n        \\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\\n            const double r = frand() * R;\\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\\n            \\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\\n                continue;\\n            }\\n            \\n            points[count] = {x, y};\\n            count++;\\n            group_cnt--;\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\\n}\\n\\n// Find the closest unclustered point to                 const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if closest point\\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\\n\\n#include <algorithm>\\n#include <chrono>\\n#include <cmath>\\n#include <cstdio>\\n#include <cstdlib>\\n#include <cstring>\\n#include <limits>\\n#include <vector>\\n\\n#include \\\"../common/results_output.hpp\\\"\\n\\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\\n\\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\\nstruct Point {\\n    double x, y;\\n};\\n\\n// Structure to represent a cluster\\nstruct Cluster {\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    int mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\\n    \\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\\n    int count = 0;\\n    \\n    while (count < N) {\\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\\n        \\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n            group_cnt = N - count;\\n        }\\n        \\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\\n            const double r = frand() * R;\\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\\n            \\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\\n                continue;\\n            }\\n            \\n            points[count] = {x, y};\\n            count++;\\n            group_cnt--;\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\\n}\\n\\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\\n                     const                 const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n continue;\\n        \\n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\\n            min_diameter = cluster_members = nullptr) {\\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    \\n    // Add seed point\\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\\n    \\n    // Iteratively add closest points\\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \\n                                             threshold, point_count);\\n        \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if requested\\n    if (cluster_members) {\\n        *cluster_members = members;\\n    }\\n    \\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\\n}\\n\\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                                  const double threshold) {\\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\\n    \\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Main clustering loop\\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\\n        int best_seed = const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\\n            \\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\\n            const int cardinality = best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\\n            }\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\\n            Cluster   \\n    // Check each cluster\\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\\n        \\n        // Check         }\\n        }\\n        \\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\\n            printf(\\\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\\\n\\\", \\n                   c,   }\\n    \\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i <     if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-n\\\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-t\\\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\\n            threshold {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792641969238,"sessionID":"ses_e9e3b4f64ffep8uPtvvZ5POMiL","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_00_wpnosnixsx6p8jc1r9xlhpjp","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/qtclustering_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r4/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - MPI Distributed Memory Parallel Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * The synthetic data set and the \"clustered\"/\"unclustered\" state are\n//     replicated on every rank (they are small compared to the candidate\n//     generation work and are deterministically identical).\n//   * In every outer iteration all unclustered points are candidate seeds.\n//     The seeds are distributed cyclically over the ranks; each rank builds\n//     the candidate clusters for its own seeds concurrently.\n//   * A reduction selects the global winner with exactly the sequential\n//     tie-break rule (largest cardinality, then smallest seed index). The\n//     winning cluster is broadcast so that every rank advances its state\n//     identically. No global synchronization is needed inside a candidate\n//     cluster build.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <numeric>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Per-rank scratch storage reused across all candidate cluster builds.\nstruct Workspace {\n    std::vector<double> max_sq;    // max squared distance to current members\n    std::vector<int> mark;         // generation stamp for \"in current cluster\"\n    int stamp = 0;\n\n    void init(int n) {\n        max_sq.resize(n);\n        mark.assign(n, 0);\n        stamp = 0;\n    }\n};\n\n// Reducer payload: (cardinality, seed). Better = larger cardinality, and on\n// a tie the smaller seed index, matching the sequential loop order exactly.\nstruct Best {\n    int card;\n    int seed;\n};\n\nstatic void best_reduce(void* invec, void* inoutvec, int* len, MPI_Datatype*) {\n    Best* in = static_cast<Best*>(invec);\n    Best* out = static_cast<Best*>(inoutvec);\n    for (int i = 0; i < *len; ++i) {\n        if (in[i].card > out[i].card ||\n            (in[i].card == out[i].card && in[i].seed < out[i].seed)) {\n            out[i] = in[i];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster.\n//\n// Only unclustered points are considered, and the maximum distance to the\n// current cluster is maintained incrementally. The candidate scan order is\n// ascending point index, exactly as in the sequential implementation, so the\n// selected points and tie-breaks are bit-for-bit identical.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const std::vector<int>& unclustered,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             Workspace& ws,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    const int U = static_cast<int>(unclustered.size());\n\n    // Advance the generation stamp instead of clearing the mark array.\n    if (++ws.stamp <= 0) {\n        std::fill(ws.mark.begin(), ws.mark.end(), 0);\n        ws.stamp = 1;\n    }\n    const int stamp = ws.stamp;\n\n    double* max_sq = ws.max_sq.data();\n    int* mark = ws.mark.data();\n    const Point* pts = points.data();\n    const int* unc = unclustered.data();\n\n    // Distance from the seed to every unclustered point.\n    const Point sp = pts[seed_point];\n    for (int idx = 0; idx < U; ++idx) {\n        const int p = unc[idx];\n        const double dx = pts[p].x - sp.x;\n        const double dy = pts[p].y - sp.y;\n        max_sq[p] = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n    }\n\n    mark[seed_point] = stamp;\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    members.reserve(64);\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    // Iteratively add the closest point that keeps the diameter below threshold.\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < U) {\n        int closest_point = -1;\n        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n        for (int idx = 0; idx < U; ++idx) {\n            const int candidate = unc[idx];\n            if (mark[candidate] == stamp) continue;\n\n            // sqrt is monotonic, so the maximum over the rounded member\n            // distances equals the rounded sqrt of the maximum squared\n            // distance. One sqrt per candidate is enough and preserves the\n            // exact floating-point comparison semantics.\n            const double max_dist = std::sqrt(max_sq[candidate]);\n            if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n                min_diameter = max_dist;\n                closest_point = candidate;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (closest_point < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n\n        mark[closest_point] = stamp;\n        members.push_back(closest_point);\n\n        // Incrementally update the maximum distance to the enlarged cluster.\n        const Point cp = pts[closest_point];\n        for (int idx = 0; idx < U; ++idx) {\n            const int p = unc[idx];\n            if (mark[p] == stamp) continue;\n            const double dx = pts[p].x - cp.x;\n            const double dy = pts[p].y - cp.y;\n            const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            if (d2 > max_sq[p]) max_sq[p] = d2;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const int cardinality = static_cast<int>(members.size());\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = std::move(members);\n    }\n    return cardinality;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI parallel).\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  const int rank,\n                                  const int nprocs,\n                                  const MPI_Datatype best_type,\n                                  const MPI_Op best_op) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered(N);\n    std::iota(unclustered.begin(), unclustered.end(), 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    Workspace ws;\n    ws.init(N);\n\n    std::vector<int> local_members;\n    std::vector<int> global_members;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered.empty()) {\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(unclustered.size());\n\n        Best local{-1, -1};\n        local_members.clear();\n\n        // Each rank builds candidate clusters for its cyclically assigned seeds.\n        for (int pos = rank; pos < U; pos += nprocs) {\n            const int seed = unclustered[pos];\n\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, points, unclustered,\n                                                             threshold, ws,\n                                                             &candidate_members);\n\n            if (cardinality > local.card ||\n                (cardinality == local.card && seed < local.seed)) {\n                local.card = cardinality;\n                local.seed = seed;\n                local_members.swap(candidate_members);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Global winner: largest cardinality, then smallest seed index.\n        Best best;\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &best, 1, best_type, best_op, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // The rank that owns the winning seed already holds its member list.\n        const int best_pos = static_cast<int>(\n            std::lower_bound(unclustered.begin(), unclustered.end(), best.seed) -\n            unclustered.begin());\n        const int root = best_pos % nprocs;\n\n        int member_count = (rank == root) ? static_cast<int>(local_members.size()) : 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        global_members.resize(member_count);\n        if (rank == root && member_count > 0) {\n            std::copy(local_members.begin(), local_members.end(), global_members.begin());\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(global_members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best.seed >= 0 && best.card > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best.seed;\n            cluster.members = global_members;\n            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (int member : global_members) {\n                clustered[member] = 1;\n            }\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered.begin(), unclustered.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                unclustered.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n\n    // Rank-local reduction type/op (identical on every rank).\n    MPI_Datatype best_type;\n    MPI_Type_contiguous(2, MPI_INT, &best_type);\n    MPI_Type_commit(&best_type);\n    MPI_Op best_op;\n    MPI_Op_create(best_reduce, 1, &best_op);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    int exit_code = 0;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (identical on all ranks).\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Op_free(&best_op);\n            MPI_Type_free(&best_type);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Op_free(&best_op);\n            MPI_Type_free(&best_type);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\",\n                   num_points, threshold);\n        }\n        MPI_Op_free(&best_op);\n        MPI_Type_free(&best_type);\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n        printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Generate synthetic data once on rank 0 and replicate it so that every\n    // rank works on bit-for-bit identical input.\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(points.data(), static_cast<int>(num_points * sizeof(Point)),\n              MPI_BYTE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Perform QT clustering\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold, rank, nprocs,\n                                                       best_type, best_op);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n        printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n\n        // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n        int total_clustered = 0;\n        int max_cluster_size = 0;\n\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n            const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n            total_clustered += size;\n            max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n        }\n\n        const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :\n            static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n\n        printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\",\n               total_clustered, num_points,\n               100.0 * double dx = pts[p].x - cp.x;\n            const double dy = pts[p].y - cp.y;\n            const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            if (d2 > max_sq[p]) max_sq[p] = d2;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const int cardinality = static_cast<int>(members.size());\n    return cardinality;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI parallel).\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  const int rank,\n                                  const int nprocs,\n                                  const MPI_Datatype best_type,\n                                  const MPI_Op best_op) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered(N);\n    std::iota(unclustered.begin(), unclustered.end(), 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    Workspace ws;\n    ws.init(N);\n\n    std::vector<int> local_members;\n    std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n    std::vector<int> global_members;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered.empty()) {\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(unclustered.size());\n\n        Best local{-1, -1};\n        local_members.clear();\n\n        // Each rank builds candidate clusters for its cyclically assigned seeds.\n        for (int pos = rank; pos < U; pos += nprocs) {\n            const int seed = unclustered[pos];\n\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, points, unclustered,\n                                                             threshold, ws,\n                                                             candidate_members);\n\n            if (cardinality > local.card ||\n                (cardinality == local.card && seed < local.seed)) {\n                local.card = cardinality;\n                local.seed = seed;\n                // Swap so both buffers keep their capacity for reuse.\n                local_members.swap(candidate_members);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Global winner: largest cardinality, then smallest seed index.\n        Best best;\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &best, 1, best_type, best_op, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // The rank that owns the winning seed already holds its member list.\n        const int best_pos = static_cast<int>(\n            std::lower_bound(unclustered.begin(), unclustered.end(), best.seed) -\n            unclustered.begin());\n        const int root = best_pos % nprocs;\n\n        int member_count = (rank == root) ? static_cast<int>(local_members.size()) : 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        global_members.resize(member_count);\n        if (rank == root && member_count > 0) {\n            std::copy(local_members.begin(), local_members.end(), global_members.begin());\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(global_members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best.seed >= 0 && best.card > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best.seed;\n            cluster.members = global_members;\n            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (int member : global_members) {\n                clustered[member] = 1;\n            }\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered.begin(), unclustered.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                unclustered.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n","exit":0,"truncated":false},"title":"sed -n '120,300p'
